    About CGN Mining                                                         關於中廣核礦業
    The Company is a company incorporated in the Cayman Islands              本公司註冊地為開曼群島，為一家在聯交
    and listed on the main board of the Stock Exchange (stock code:          所主板上市的公司（股票代號：01164），
    01164). The principal activities of the Company are development and      主要業務為核能企業使用的天然鈾資源的
    trading of natural uranium resources for the use of nuclear energy       開發與貿易。本公司定位為中廣核集團海
    companies. The Company positions itself as the sole overseas uranium     外鈾資源開發的唯一平台，致力於為核電
    development platform for the CGN Group, which strives to provide         業主提供優質可靠的天然鈾產品，為清潔
    nuclear power plants owners with high-quality and reliable natural       環境貢獻力量。
    uranium products, and contributes to a clean environment.


    The Company adheres to its core value of “Doing Things Right in One      本公司始終堅持「一次把事情做好」的核心
    Go” and takes “Safety First, Quality Foremost, Pursuing Excellence” as   價值觀，並以「安全第一，質量第一，追
    its working principle.                                                   求卓越」為工作原則。

to or even lower than the production cost for the past few years, some     於生產成本線位置徘徊，出於生存及止損
major producers have announced production cuts or suspensions for          考慮，部分主要生產商宣佈減產、停產，
survival and cutting loss, which created possibilities for the recovery.   為天然鈾價格的回升創造了可能。作為天

uranium price has basically changed the downward trend and                 價格已基本改變下探格局，開始觸底反

return to its original value. For CGN Mining, the upturn means new         而言，向好的形勢意味著新的發展機遇，
development opportunities and also brings new challenges to us.            但同時也為我們帶來新的挑戰。

     Chief Executive Officer’s Statement
     首席執行官致辭

     Dear Shareholders,                                                           各位股東，


     On behalf of the management, I present to you the overall operation of       本人謹代表管理層向各位股東報告本公司
     the Company in 2018.                                                         2018 年度整體經營情況。


     The spot price of natural uranium rebounded from the bottom in               2018年天然鈾現貨價格從底部反彈，全年
     2018 and ended with an increase of over 30% throughout the year.             漲幅超過 30%。儘管 2019 年市場供需基
     Although the market is still oversupply in 2019, with the imbalance          本面仍處於供大於求，但供需失衡狀況已
     between supply and demand improved significantly, the international          明顯改善，預期2019年國際鈾價會進一步
     uranium price is expected to increase further in 2019. Under this            上漲。在此大環境下，我們主動出擊對既
     circumstance, we took the initiative to carry out lean management of         有業務進行精益化管理，力爭提升利潤水
     existing businesses and strived to improve profits, while promoting          平，同時力推中廣核國際銷售公司 100%
     the acquisition of 100% equity of CGN Global to further expand the           股權的收購，以進一步拓展公司業務。報
     Company’s business. During the Reporting Period, the Company                 告期內，本公司繼續實現了盈利，且較
     continued to record a profit, representing a substantial increase as         2017年大幅增長。作為首席執行官，我要
     compared to 2017. As the chief executive officer, I would like to thank      感謝各位股東對我們的支持與理解，同時
     all Shareholders for their support and understanding, and also to thank      也要感謝全體員工面對挑戰的不懈奮鬥。
     all employees for their unremitting endeavours. I will deliver you a         以下我將向各位股東簡要報告我們在2018

Financial capital                                                           財務資本
The Company adopts prudent capital and treasury policy and goals.           本公司採納了謹慎的資金與財政政策及目
During the Reporting Period, the Company’s operating fund was               標。報告期間，運營所需的資金主要來自
mainly from the cash generated from operating activities. The capital       經營活動產生的現金。本公司現金需求主
needs requirements of the Company mainly come from the possible             要包括為收購天然鈾資源而可能發生的併
acquisition expenses of acquiring natural uranium resources and funds       購開支以及運營的資金需求。

Responsibilities of the Board                                             董事會職責

As the decision-marking body of the Company, the Board takes charge       董事會作為公司的決策機構，就公司治理
of the corporate governance function. The major responsibilities of the   負有責任和義務。董事會的職責主要包括

     Board Diversity policy                                                     董事多元化政策

     The Board adopted the “Board Diversity Policy”. The Company                董事會已採納董事會成員多元化政策。本
     recognises the merits of Board diversity and sees the edge of              公司認可董事會成員多元化的優點，並已
     Board diversity in driving competitiveness. In making up the Board’s       看到董事會成員多元化在提高競爭力方面
     composition, the Board considers various skills, regional and industry     的優勢。在董事會成員的組成上，董事會
     experience, background, gender and other attributes of the Board           將考慮董事會成員的不同技能、區域和行
     members. All Board appointments are based on the talent, skill and         業經驗、背景、性別以及其他特點。所有
     experience of the individual, as well as gender diversity, so as to meet   的董事會任命均基於才幹、技能、經驗以
     the operational needs.                                                     及性別差異等，以適應工作需要。

Internal Control                                                         內部監控

The Board has overall responsibility for setting up and maintaining      董事會整體負責建立與維持公司內部監控
effective internal control system and for reviewing its effectiveness,   體系的有效性，並不斷檢討其效力以保障
to safeguard the Group’s assets and Shareholders’ interest. The          本集團的資產及股東的利益。公司各級管
management of the Company at each level assesses and reports             理層通過對內部監控系統的檢討來評估內
on the adequacy and effectiveness of the internal control system by      部監控系統的充足性和有效性。內部監控
performing reviews. Internal control department reports to the audit     部門每年度向審核委員會彙報一次有關內
committee once a year on key findings regarding internal controls.       部監控的主要調查結果，審核委員會向董
Audit committee, in turn, communicates any material issues regarding     事會彙報有關內部監控系統中存在的重大
internal control system to the Board.                                    事項和問題。


During the Reporting Period, the audit committee conducted a review      報告期內，審核委員會已檢討內部監控系
on the effectiveness of the internal control system, and no serious      統的有效性，並無發現任何嚴重不足。因
deficiencies were identified. Therefore, the Board considered that the   此，董事會認為本集團的內部監控系統有
internal control systems of the Group are effective, and complied with   效且符合企業管治守則的守則條文。
the provisions of the Corporate Governance Code.

FINANCIAL STATEMENTS

The Directors acknowledge their responsibility for the preparation       董事確認其對於編製本集團財務報表應負

policies have been used and applied consistently, and reasonable and     策並貫徹地應用，並已作出合理和審慎的
prudent judgments and estimates have been made. The Board is not         判斷與估計。董事會並不知悉任何或可能
aware of any material uncertainties relating to events or conditions     對本集團持續經營的能力構成重大疑問的
which may cast significant doubt over the Group’s ability to continue    事件或情況有關的任何重大不明朗因素存
as a going concern. Accordingly, the Board has continued to adopt the    在。因此，董事會於編製財務報表時已持
going concern basis in preparing the financial statements.               續採用持續經營基準。

      Auditor’s Responsibilities for the                                          核數師對審核綜合財務報表之
      Audit of the Consolidated Financial                                         責任
      Statements
      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the         我們的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否不
      consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from material         存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤

     Exchange”). Its parent is 中國鈾業發展有限公司 (China                            公司為中國鈾業發展有限公司（「中
     Uranium Development Company Limited) (“China Uranium                   國鈾業發展」），該公司為一家於香
     Development”), a company incorporated in Hong Kong and a               港註冊成立的公司，並為中廣核鈾
     wholly-owned subsidiary of 中廣核鈾業發展有限公司 (CGNPC                          業發展有限公司（「中廣核鈾業發
     Uranium Resources Co. Ltd.) (“CGNPC-URC”), which is in                 展」）的全資附屬公司，後者為中國
     turn a subsidiary of 中國廣核集團有限公司 (China General                         廣核集團有限公司（「中廣核集團公
     Nuclear Power Corporation) (“CGNPC”). CGNPC is the ultimate            司」）的附屬公司。中廣核集團公司
     parent of the Company. Both CGNPC-URC and CGNPC were                   為本公司的最終母公司。中廣核鈾
     established in the PRC and state-owned enterprises.                    業發展及中廣核集團公司均於中國

     HKAS 18 Revenue and related interpretations and it applies                    港會計準則第11號建築合約、香港

     whether, how much and when revenue is recognised. The                        準的範圍。新準則確立一個五步模
     Group has elected to adopt the modified retrospective                        式，以釐定是否確認收入、確認多

     as at the date of initial application (i.e. 1 January 2018) with             選擇就首次應用日期（即 2018 年 1
     the cumulative effect of initially applying HKFRS 15 as an                    月1日）尚未完成的客戶合約採納經
     adjustment to the opening balance of retained earnings                        修訂追溯法，而首次應用香港財務
     and comparative information is not restated. Accordingly,                     報告準則第15號的累積影響會作為
     certain comparative information may not be comparable                         對保留盈利期初結餘的調整，乃由

             financial position of the Group.                                            並無重大影響。


             HKFRS 16 Leases                                                             香港財務報告準則第 16 號－租賃

             HKFRS 16 provides a comprehensive model for the                             香港財務報告準則第16號為租賃安
             identification of lease arrangements and their treatment in the             排之識別以及其於出租人及承租人
             financial statements of both lessors and lessees.                           財務報表之處理方式提供一個綜合
                                                                                         模型。


             In respect of the lessee accounting, the standard introduces a              就承租人會計處理而言，該準則引
             single lessee accounting model, requiring lessees to recognise              入單一承租人會計處理模式，規定
             assets and liabilities for all leases with the lease term of more           承租人就租賃期超過十二個月的所
             than 12 months, unless the underlying asset has a low value.                有租賃確認資產及負債，惟相關資
                                                                                         產價值較低則除外。


             At the commencement date of the lease, the lessee is required               於租賃開始日期，承租人須按成本
             to recognise a right-of-use asset at cost, which consists of                確認使用權資產，包括租賃負債
             the amount of the initial measurement of the lease liability,               之初始計量金額，加上於開始日期
             plus any lease payments made to the lessor at or before the                 或之前向出租人作出之任何租賃付
             commencement date less any lease incentives received, the                   款，扣除任何已收取之租賃優惠、
             initial estimate of restoration costs and any initial direct costs          初始估計修復成本及承租人所產生
             incurred by the lessee. A lease liability is initially recognised at        之任何初始直接成本。租賃負債初
             the present value of the lease payments that are not paid at that           步按於租賃付款尚未支付當日之現
             date.                                                                       值確認。

     AMENDMENTS HONG KONG FINANCIAL                                             務報告準則（「香港財務報

                                                                                    ）續）
     (Continued)

     HKFRS 16 Leases (Continued)                                                香港財務報告準則第 16 號－租賃
                                                                                （續）

     Subsequently, the right-of-use asset is measured at cost                   其後，使用權資產按成本減任何累
     less any accumulated depreciation and any accumulated                      計折舊及任何累計減值虧損計量，
     impairment losses, and adjusted for any remeasurement of                   並就任何重新計量之租賃負債作出
     the lease liability. Lease liability is subsequently measured              調整。租賃負債之其後計量方式為
     by increasing the carrying amount to reflect interest on the               透過增加賬面值以反映租賃負債之
     lease liability, reducing the carrying amount to reflect the lease         利息，透過削減賬面值以反映所作
     payment made, and remeasuring the carrying amount to reflect               出之租賃付款，以及透過重新計量
     any reassessment or lease modifications or to reflect revised in-          賬面值以反映任何重新評估或租賃
     substance fixed lease payments. Depreciation and impairment                修訂或反映經修訂之實質固定租賃
     expenses, if any, on the right-of-use asset will be charged to             付款。使用權資產之折舊及減值費
     profit or loss following the requirements of HKAS 16 Property,             用（如有）將按照香港會計準則第16
     Plant and Equipment, while interest accrual on lease liability will        號－物業、廠房及設備之規定計入
     be charged to profit or loss.                                              損益，而租賃負債之應計利息將於
                                                                                損益內扣除。


     In respect of the lessor accounting, HKFRS 16 substantially                就出租人會計處理而言，香港財務
     carries forward the lessor accounting requirements in HKAS 17              報告準則第16號實質上沿用香港會
     Leases. Accordingly, a lessor continues to classify its leases             計準則第17號的出租人會計規定。
     as operating leases or finance leases, and to account for those            因此，出租人繼續將其租賃分類為
     two types of leases differently.                                           經營租賃或融資租賃，並以不同方
                                                                                式將此兩類租賃入賬。


     HKFRS 16 will supersede the current lease standards including              香港財務報告準則第16號生效後，
     HKAS 17 Leases and the related Interpretations when it                     將取代現有租賃準則，包括香港會
     becomes effective.                                                         計準則第17號－租賃及相關詮釋。

3.	SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     3.   主要會計政策
   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in              綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公
   accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition,                會頒佈之香港財務報告準則編製。
   the consolidated financial statements include applicable                 此外，綜合財務報表包括聯交所證
   disclosures required by the Rules Governing the Listing                  券上市規則及香港《公司條例》適用
   of Securities on the Stock Exchange and the Hong Kong                    之披露規定。
   Companies Ordinance.


   The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on              綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編
   the historical cost basis, except for investment properties that         製，惟投資物業則按公允值計算。
   are measured at fair values.


   Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the              歷史成本一般根據交換商品及服務
   consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                  提供代價之公允值計算。


   Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset          公允值為市場參與者於現時市況下
   or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between        於計量日在主要（或最有利）市場因
   market participants in the principal (or most advantageous)              出售資產而收取或因轉讓負債而支
   market at the measurement date under current market                      付之價格（即平倉價），不論該價格
   conditions (i.e. an exit price) regardless of whether that price         是否直接觀察或利用其他估值方法
   is directly observable or estimated using another valuation              估計。公允值計量詳情闡述於下文
   technique. Details of fair value measurement are explained in            載列之會計政策。主要會計政策載
   the accounting policies set out below.                                   於下文。


   The principal accounting policies are set out below.                     主要會計政策載於下文。


   Basis of consolidation                                                   綜合基準

   The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial          綜合財務報表包括本公司及受本公
   statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                 司所控制實體（即其附屬公司）之財
   Company (i.e. its subsidiaries). If a subsidiary prepares its            務報表。倘附屬公司編製其財務報
   financial statements using accounting policies other than                表所採用之會計政策並非綜合財務
   those adopted in the consolidated financial statements for like          報表中就類似情況下之類似交易及
   transactions and events in similar circumstances, appropriate            事件採用之會計政策，則應在編製
   adjustments are made to that subsidiary’s financial statements           綜合財務報表時在該附屬公司之財
   in preparing the consolidated financial statements to ensure             務報表中作出適當調整以確保與本
   conformity with the Group’s accounting policies.                         集團所採取之會計政策相一致。

             Control is achieved where the Group has: (i) the power over                  倘本集團 ( i ) 對投資對象擁有權力；
             the investee; (ii) exposure, or rights, to variable returns from its         (ii) 對參與投資對象而獲得之可變回
             involvement with the investee; and (iii) the ability to use its power        報須承擔風險或擁有權利；及(iii)可
             over the investee to affect the amount of the Group’s returns.               對投資對象行使其權力以影響本集
             When the Group has less than a majority of the voting rights of              團之回報金額，則獲得控制權。倘
             an investee, power over the investee may be obtained through:                本集團於投資對象之投票權未能佔
             (i) a contractual arrangement with other vote holders; (ii) rights           大多數，則可根據所有相關因素及
             arising from other contractual arrangements; (iii) the Group’s               情況通過以下途徑取得對投資對象
             voting rights and potential voting rights; or (iv) a combination of          之權力：( i ) 與其他投票權持有人訂
             the above, based on all relevant facts and circumstances.                    立合約安排；(ii) 其他合約安排產生
                                                                                          之權利；(iii)本集團之投票權及潛在
                                                                                          投票權；或 (iv) 上述途徑之組合。


             The Company reassesses whether it controls an investee if                    倘相關事實及情況表明上述控制權
             facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one               之相關元素中一項或多項元素發生
             or more of these elements of control stated above.                           變動，則本公司會重估是否其對投
                                                                                          資對象具有控制權。


             Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Company                        當本公司獲得附屬公司之控制權
             obtains control of the subsidiary and ceases when the Group                  時，附屬公司之綜合開始而當本集
             loses control of the subsidiary.                                             團失去附屬公司之控制權時則附屬
                                                                                          公司之綜合終止。


             Income and expenses of subsidiaries are included in                          於本集團取得控制權日期直至本集

             comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control                   屬公司之收支計入綜合損益及其他
             until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.              全面收入表。


             Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive                     附屬公司之損益及其他全面收入之
             income of subsidiaries are attributed to the owners of                       各部分歸屬於本公司擁有人及非
             the Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total                      控股權益。附屬公司之全面收入總
             comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed to the                    額歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權
             owners of the Company and to the non-controlling interests                   益，即使此情況導致非控股權益產
             even if this results in the non-controlling interests having a               生虧絀結餘。
             deficit balance.


             All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses             與本集團內實體間之往來交易有關
             and cash flows relating to transactions between entities of the              的資產及負債、權益、收入、開支
             Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                               及現金流量於綜合時全數抵銷。

   Investments in an associate and a joint venture                            於聯營及合營企業之投資

   An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant             聯營企業指本集團能對其行使重大
   influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the        影響力。重大影響力指可參與受投
   financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is            資公司之財務及營運決策但不是控
   not control or joint control of those policies.                            制或共同控制該等政策之權力。


   A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties that            合營企業指一項共同安排，對安排
   have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net               擁有共同控制權之訂約方據此對安
   assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is the contractually        排之資產淨值擁有權利。共同控制
   agreed sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists only             是指按照合約約定對某項安排所共
   when decisions about the relevant activities require unanimous             有的控制，共同控制僅在當相關活
   consent of the parties sharing control.                                    動要求共同享有控制權之各方作出
                                                                              一致同意之決定時存在。


   The Group’s investments in an associate and a joint venture are            本集團於聯營及合營企業的投資於
   accounted for in the consolidated financial statements using               綜合財務報表入賬內採用權益法。
   the equity method. Under the equity method, investments in                 根據權益法，於聯營及合營企業的
   an associate and a joint venture are initially recognised at cost.         投資初始按成本確認。本集團應佔
   The Group’s share of the profit or loss and changes in the other           聯營及合營企業的損益及其他全面
   comprehensive income of the associate and joint venture are                收入之變動分別於收購日期後於益
   recognised in profit or loss and other comprehensive income                損及其他全面收入內確認。倘本集
   respectively after the date of acquisition. If the Group’s share           團應佔聯營及合營企業的虧損等於
   of losses of an associate or a joint venture equals or exceeds             或超過本集團於該聯營及合營企業
   its interest in the associate or joint venture, which determined           的權益（使用權益法釐定連同實質
   using the equity method together with any long-term interests              屬於本集團於聯營及合營企業的投
   that, in substance, form part of the Group’s net investment                資淨值的任何長期權益）時，本集
   in the associate or joint venture, the Group discontinues                  團不再確認其分佔的進一步虧損。
   recognising its share of further losses. Additional losses are             本集團僅在已招致法定或推定責任
   provided for, and a liability is recognised, only to the extent            或代表聯營及合營企業支付款項的
   that the Group has incurred legal or constructive obligations or           情況下確認額外虧損或負債。
   made payments on behalf of the associate or joint venture.

   in similar circumstances, adjustments are made to make the                 政策有別於本集團所採納者，則在

   (Continued)

   Gains and losses resulting from transactions between the                 本集團與其合營及聯營企業進行交
   Group and its associate or joint venture are recognised in               易所得之損益，只會在於該聯營
   consolidated financial statements only to the extent of unrelated        及合營企業並無關連之投資者權益
   investors’ interests in the associate or joint venture. The              的情況下，方會在綜合財務報表確
   Group’s share in the associate’s or joint venture’s gains or             認。本集團應佔聯營及合營企業來
   losses resulting from these transactions is eliminated.                  自該等交易之損益予以抵銷。


   Investments in subsidiaries                                              於附屬公司之投資

   financial position of the Company at cost less accumulated               況表按成本減累計減值虧損列賬
   impairment loss, if any.                                                 （如有）。


   Property, plant and equipment                                            物業、廠房及設備

   Property, plant and equipment including leasehold land and               物業、廠房及設備包括持作生產或
   buildings (classified as finance leases) held for use in the             供應商品或服務或作行政用途之租
   production or supply of goods or services, or for administrative         賃土地及樓宇（分類為融資租賃），
   purposes, are stated in the consolidated statement of financial          乃按成本值減其後累計折舊及其後
   position at cost less subsequent accumulated depreciation and            累計減值虧損（如有）列於綜合財務
   subsequent accumulated impairment losses, if any.                        狀況表。


   Depreciation is recognised so as to allocate the cost of items           物業、廠房及設備項目之折舊按其
   of property, plant and equipment less their residual values over         估計可使用年期，以直線法分配其
   their estimated useful lives, using the straight-line method. The        減除估計剩餘價值後之成本計算。
   estimated useful lives, residual values and depreciation method          估計使用年期、剩餘價值及折舊方
   are reviewed at the end of each reporting period, with the effect        法乃於各報告期間結束時檢討，估
   of any changes in estimate accounted for on a prospective                計變動之影響按前瞻基準入賬。
   basis.


   An item of property, plant and equipment is derecognised upon            物業、廠房及設備項目於出售時或
   disposal or when no future economic benefits are expected                當預期不會因持續使用資產產生未
   to arise from the continued use of the asset. Any gain or loss           來經濟利益時終止確認。因物業、
   arising on the disposal or retirement of an item of property,            廠房及設備項目被出售或報廢而產
   plant and equipment is determined as the difference between              生之任何盈虧乃按該項資產之出售
   the sales proceeds and the carrying amount of the asset and is           所得款項與賬面值之差額計算，並
   recognised in profit or loss.                                            於損益內確認。

   (Continued)

   Impairment losses on tangible assets (Continued)                                 （續）
                                                                            有形資產之減值虧損

   When it is not possible to estimate the recoverable amount               倘無法估計個別資產之可收回金
   of an individual asset, the Group estimates the recoverable              額，本集團估計資產所屬現金產生
   amount of the cash-generating unit to which the asset belongs.           單位之可收回金額。如可識別合理
   When a reasonable and consistent basis of allocation can be              及一致之分配基準，則公司資產亦
   identified, corporate assets are also allocated to individual            被分配至個別現金產生單位，或於
   cash-generating units, or otherwise they are allocated to the            其他情況下彼等被分配至已識別合
   smallest group of cash-generating units for which a reasonable           理及一致分配基準之現金產生單位
   and consistent allocation basis can be identified.                       之最小組合。


   Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs                可收回金額為公允值減去處置成本
   of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the             及使用價值兩者中之較高者。於評
   estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present              估使用價值時，估計未來現金流量
   value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market         乃以稅前貼現率貼現至現值，該貼
   assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific            現率能反映當前市場所評估之貨幣
   to the asset for which the estimates of future cash flows have           時間值及資產特定風險（未來現金
   not been adjusted.                                                       流量估計尚未就此作出調整）。


   If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a cash-generating unit)        倘估計一項資產（或現金產生單位）
   is estimated to be less than its carrying amount, the carrying           之可收回金額低於其賬面值，則該
   amount of the asset (or cash-generating unit) is reduced to              項資產（或現金產生單位）之賬面值
   its recoverable amount. An impairment loss is recognised                 即減至其可收回金額。減值虧損即
   immediately in profit or loss.                                           時於損益中確認。


   Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying             於其後撥回減值虧損時，資產（或
   amount of the asset (or cash-generating unit) is increased to            現金產生單位）之賬面值將調高至
   the revised estimate of its recoverable amount, but so that              其經修訂估計之可收回金額，惟經
   the increased carrying amount does not exceed the carrying               增加之賬面值不得高於在過往年
   amount that would have been determined had no impairment                 度 並無確認減值虧損下就該資產
   loss been recognised for the asset (or cash-generating unit) in          （或現金產生單位）原應釐定之賬面
   prior years. A reversal of an impairment loss is recognised as           值。撥回之減值虧損即時於收入中
   income immediately.                                                      確認。

             Revenue recognition                                                          收入確認

             Policy applicable to the year ended 31 December 2018 (with                   截至 2018 年 12 月 31 日止年度應用
             application of HKFRS 15)                                                     之政策（應用香港財務報告準則第
                                                                                          15 號）

             Revenue is recognised to depict the transfer of promised                     確認收入乃描述已承諾服務對客戶
             services to customers in an amount that reflects the                         的轉移，而確認金額乃反映實體預
             consideration to which the entity expects to be entitled in                  期從該等服務應得的代價。具體而
             exchange for those services. Specifically, the Group uses a                  言，本集團使用五個步驟來確認收
             5-step approach to revenue recognition:                                      入：


             •       Step 1: Identify the contract(s) with customer                       •       第 一 步： 識 別 與 客 戶 訂 立
                                                                                                  的合約


             •       Step 2: Identify the performance obligations in the                  •       第 二 步： 識 別 合 約 中 的 履
                     contract                                                                     約責任


             •       Step 3: Determine the transaction price                              •       第三步：釐定交易價


             •       Step 4: Allocate the transaction price to the                        •       第 四 步： 將 交 易 價 分 配 至
                     performance obligations in the contract                                      合約中的履約責任


             •       Step 5: Recognise revenue when (or as) the entity                    •       第 五 步： 於 實 體 完 成 履 約
                     satisfies a performance obligation                                           責任時確認收入


             The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                      本集團於完成履約責任時確認收
             obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods                    入，即於特定履約責任相關的貨品
             underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred              的「控制權」轉讓予客戶時。
             to customers.


             A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a                  履約責任指個別的貨品或服務（或
             bundle of goods or services) that is district or a series of distinct        一組貨品或服務）或一系列大致相
             goods or services that are substantially same.                               同的個別貨品或服務。

             Taxation                                                                  稅項

             Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                所得稅支出指應付即期稅項及遞延
             payable and deferred tax.                                                 稅項之總和。


             The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.        應付即期稅項乃按本年度應課稅溢
             Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported in the          利計算。應課稅溢利與綜合損益表
             consolidated statement of profit or loss because it excludes              中所報除稅前溢利不同，原因為前
             items of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in              者不包括在其他年度應課稅或可扣
             other years and it further excludes items that are never taxable          稅收入或開支，且不包括毋須課稅
             or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax is calculated        或不可扣稅之項目。本集團之即期
             using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively enacted           稅項負債乃按報告期間結束前已頒
             by the end of the reporting period.                                       佈或實際已頒佈之稅率計算。


             Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between               遞延稅項乃就於綜合財務報表所示
             the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated        資產及負債之賬面值與計算應課
             financial statements and the corresponding tax bases used                 稅溢利所用相應稅基間臨時差額確
             in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are        認。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課
             generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences.               稅臨時差額確認，而遞延稅項資產
             Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible           則於可能可動用應課稅溢利以抵銷
             temporary difference to the extent that it is probable that               可扣減臨時差額之情況下確認。若
             taxable profits will be available against which those deductible          於一項交易中，因商譽或因業務合
             temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax assets           併以外原因初步確認其他資產及負
             and liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference            債而引致不會影響應課稅溢利亦不

             a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the
             accounting profit.

   Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary                 遞延稅項負債乃按附屬公司及聯營
   differences associated with investments in subsidiaries, an                   企業投資及合營企業之應課稅臨時
   associate and a joint venture, except where the Group is able                 差額確認，惟倘本集團可控制臨時
   to control the reversal of the temporary difference and it is                 差額撥回及臨時差額未必於可見將
   probable that the temporary difference will not reverse in the                來撥回之情況則除外。有關該等投
   foreseeable future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible               資及權益之可扣減臨時差額所產生
   temporary differences associated with such investments and                    遞延稅項資產，僅於具有足夠應課
   interests are only recognised to the extent that it is probable               稅溢利可動用暫時差異之利益，且
   that there will be sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise        預期於可見將來撥回時確認。
   the benefits of the temporary differences and they are expected
   to reverse in the foreseeable future.


   The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the                 遞延稅項資產的賬面值於每一報告
   end of each reporting period and reduced to the extent that it is             期末予以審閱，如不可能再有足夠
   no longer probable that sufficient taxable profits will be available          應課稅溢利可供收回全部或部分資
   to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.                            產，則將削減遞延稅項的賬面值。


   Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax rates             遞延稅項資產及負債乃根據於報告
   that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is            期間結束時已頒佈或實際已頒佈之
   settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rates (and tax laws)           稅率（及稅法），按預期於清償負債
   that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of                 或變現資產期間適用之稅率計算。
   the reporting period.


   The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects               遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反映本
   the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                     集團預期於報告期間結束時收回或
   which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period, to               償還資產及負債賬面值產生之稅務
   recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and liabilities.          後果。


   For the purposes of measuring deferred tax liabilities or deferred            就計量採用公允值模式計量的投資
   tax assets for investment properties that are measured using                  物業的遞延稅項負債或遞延稅項資
   the fair value model, the carrying amounts of such properties                 產而言，該等物業的賬面值乃假設
   are presumed to be recovered entirely through sale, unless the                可透過銷售全部收回，除非該假設
   presumption is rebutted. The presumption is rebutted when                     不再成立。當投資物業可予折舊及
   the investment property is depreciable and is held within a                   於本集團的業務模式（其業務目標
   business model whose objective is to consume substantially all                是隨時間而非透過銷售消耗投資物
   of the economic benefits embodied in the investment property                  業所包含的絕大部分經濟利益）內
   over time, rather than through sale. If the presumption is                    持有時，有關假設會不成立。倘有
   rebutted, deferred tax liabilities and deferred tax assets for such           關假設不成立，則上述投資物業的
   investment properties are measured in accordance with the                     遞延稅項負債及遞延稅項資產根據
   general principles above.                                                     上述一般原則計量。

             Leasing                                                                   租賃

             Leases are classified as finance leases whenever the terms                當租約條款將擁有權之絕大部分風
             of the lease transfer substantially all the risks and rewards             險及回報轉移予承租人時，租約分
             of ownership to the lessee. All other leases are classified as            類為融資租賃。所有其他租賃均分
             operating leases.                                                         類為經營租賃。


             The Group as lessor                                                       本集團作為出租人

             Rental income from operating leases is recognised in profit or            經營租賃之租金收入於相關租賃年
             loss on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant lease.        期內以直線法在損益確認。


             The Group as lessee                                                       本集團作為承租人

             Operating lease payments are recognised as an expense on a                經營租賃付款以直線法，按相關租
             straight-line basis over the term of the relevant lease.                  期確認為開支。


             Leasehold land and building                                               租賃土地及樓宇

             When a lease includes both land and building elements, the                當租賃包括土地及樓宇部分時，本
             Group assesses the classification of each element as a finance            集團按擁有各部分之大部分風險及
             or an operating lease separately based on the assessment as               回報是否已轉移至本集團之評估，
             to whether substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to          將各部分獨立分類為融資租賃或經
             ownership of each element have been transferred to the Group,             營租賃，除非兩項要素均為經營租
             unless it is clear that both elements are operating leases in             賃，在該情況下整項租賃會分類為
             which case the entire lease is classified as an operating lease.          經營租賃。尤其是，最低租賃付款
             Specifically, the minimum lease payments (including any lump-             （包括任何一次性預付款）在租約期
             sum upfront payments) are allocated between the land and the              開始時，按租約之土地及樓宇部分
             building elements in proportion to the relative fair values of the        之租賃權益相關公允值按比例分配
             leasehold interests in the land element and building element of           於土地及樓宇部分。
             the lease at the inception of the lease.


             To the extent the allocation of the lease payments can be                 倘租賃付款能可靠分配，列作經營
             made reliably, interest in leasehold land that is accounted for           租賃之租賃土地權益，惟已根據公
             as an operating lease, except for those that are classified and           允值模式分類及列作投資物業者則
             accounted for as investment properties under the fair value               除外。當租賃付款不能在土地及樓
             model. When the lease payment cannot be allocated reliably                宇部分之間可靠地分配時，整項租
             between the land and building elements, the entire lease is               賃一般被分類為融資租賃，並作為
             generally classified as a finance lease and accounted for as              物業、廠房及設備列賬。
             property, plant and equipment.

                                                                                                      （未經調整）。

   Financial assets                                                           金融資產

   assets that require delivery of assets within the time frame               定之時限內交付之金融資產。
   established by regulation or convention in the marketplace.


   All recognised financial assets are subsequently measured in               所有已確認金融資產其後全數按已
   their entirety at either amortised cost or fair value, depending           攤銷成本或公允值計量，視乎金
   on the classification of the financial assets. Financial assets are        融資產之分類而定。金融資產於初
   classified, at initial recognition, as subsequently measured at            步確認時分類為其後按攤銷成本計
   amortised cost.                                                            量。

   depends on the financial asset’s contractual cash flow                     於金融資產的合約現金流量特色及
   characteristics and the Group’s business model for managing                本集團管理該等特色的商業模式。
   them.

   subsequently measured at amortised cost:                                   金）於其後按攤銷成本計量：


   •       the financial asset is held within a business model                •     該金融資產以收取合約現
           whose objective is to hold financial assets in order to                  金流量為目的之商業模式

           outstanding.                                                             本金之利息。


   Financial asset at amortised cost are measured using the                   按攤銷成本計量之金融資產以按實

             Amortised cost and effective interest method                                攤銷成本及實際利率法


             The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                實際利率法乃計算債務工具攤銷成
             amortised cost of a debt instrument and of allocating interest              本及按有關期間攤分利息收入之方
             income over the relevant period.                                            法。


             For financial assets other than purchased or originated                     對於除購入或承擔信貸已減值的
             credit‑impaired financial assets (i.e. assets that are                      金融資產（即於初次確認時承擔信
             credit‑impaired on initial recognition), the effective interest rate        貸已減值的金融資產）外的金融資
             is the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts           產，實際利率法乃按債務工具預計
             (including all fees and points paid or received that form an                存續期或（如適用）較短期間，準
             integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and         備貼現估計未來現金收入（包括構
             other premiums or discounts) excluding expected credit losses,              成實際利率不可或缺部分之一切已
             through the expected life of the debt instrument, or, where                 付或已收費用及點數、交易成本及
             appropriate, a shorter period, to the gross carrying amount                 其他溢價或折讓，不包括預期信貸
             of the debt instrument on initial recognition. For purchased or             虧損）至其初步確認時債務工具之
             originated credit‑impaired financial assets, a credit‑adjusted              賬面總值之利率。對於購入或承擔
             effective interest rate is calculated by discounting the estimated          信貸已減值的金融資產，信貸調整
             future cash flows, including expected credit losses, to the                 實際利率法乃按估計未來現金流量
             amortised cost of the debt instrument on initial recognition.               （包括預期信貸虧損）貼現至初始
                                                                                         確認時債務工具攤銷成本的方式計
                                                                                         算。


             The amortised cost of a financial asset is the amount at which              金融資產的攤銷成本指金融資產於
             the financial asset is measured at initial recognition minus the            初始確認時計量的金額減去本金
             principal repayments, plus the cumulative amortisation using                還款，加上初始金額與到期金額間
             the effective interest method of any difference between that                的任何差額使用實際利率法計算的
             initial amount and the maturity amount, adjusted for any loss               累計攤銷（就任何虧損撥備作出調
             allowance. The gross carrying amount of a financial asset is the            整）。金融資產的賬面總值指金融
             amortised cost of a financial asset before adjusting for any loss           資產就任何虧損撥備作出調整前的
             allowance.                                                                  攤銷成本。

             Significant increase in credit risk                                       信貸風險顯著上升


             In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly          評估信貸風險是否自初始確認顯著
             since initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a               上升時，本集團比較金融工具於報
             default occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting         告日期發生違約的風險與金融工具
             date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial                於初始確認日期發生違約的風險。
             instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In making               作出評估時，本集團會考慮合理及
             this assessment, the Group considers both quantitative and                可靠的定量及定性資料，包括過往
             qualitative information that is reasonable and supportable,               經驗及無需付出過多成本或努力即
             including historical experience and forward-looking information           可得的前瞻性資料。
             that is available without undue cost or effort.


             In particular, the following information is taken into account            具體而言，在評估信貸風險是否顯
             when assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:           著增加時會考慮以下資料：


             •       an actual or expected significant deterioration in the            •   金融工具外部（如有）或內
                     financial instrument’s external (if available) or internal            部信貸評級的實際或預期
                     credit rating;                                                        顯著惡化；


             •       existing or forecast adverse changes in business,                 •   商 業、 金 融 或 經 濟 情 況 出
                     financial or economic conditions that are expected to                 現預計會導致債務人償債
                     cause a significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to               能力顯著下降的現有或預
                     meet its debt obligations;                                            期不利變化；


             •       an actual or expected significant deterioration in the            •   債務人經營業績實際或預
                     operating results of the debtor;                                      期顯著惡化；


             •       an actual or expected significant adverse change in the           •   債 務 人 的 監 管、 經 濟 或 技
                     regulatory, economic, or technological environment of                 術環境有實際或預期的顯
                     the debtor that results in a significant decrease in the              著 不 利 變 動， 導 致 債 務 人
                     debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations.                        償還債項的能力顯著下降。

                                                                                 作別論。

   determined to have low credit risk if i) it has a low risk of default,        有較低之信貸風險假若 i ) 其違約風
   ii) the borrower has a strong capacity to meet its contractual                險較低；i i ) 借款人在短期內絕對有

   in economic and business conditions in the longer term may,                   以及 i i i ) 經濟和商業條件長遠之不
   but will not necessarily, reduce the ability of the borrower to               利變化，不一定會降低借款人履行
   fulfill its contractual cash flow obligations. The Group considers            合約現金流義務之能力。本集團認
   a financial asset to have low credit risk when the asset has                  為，若根據眾所周知的定義，金融
   external credit rating of ‘investment grade’ in accordance with               資產的外部信貸評級為「投資級」
   the globally understood definition or if an external rating is                或（不存在外部評級時）內部評級為
   not available, the asset has an internal rating of ‘performing’.              「履約級」，則該金融資產具有較低
   Performing means that the counterparty has a strong financial                 信貸風險。履約級指對手的財政狀
   position and there is no past due amounts.                                    況穩健及沒有逾期款項。


   The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the criteria                本集團定期監控用以識別信貸風險

   Credit-impaired financial assets                                          金融資產之信貸減值


   A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events              當發生一件或以上事件對金融資產
   that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash               的估計未來現金流量造成不利影響
   flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a              時，該金融資產出現信貸減值。金
   financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable data               融資產出現信貸減值的證據包括有
   about the following events:                                               關下列事件的可觀察數據：


   •      significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the              •   發行人或借貸人遭遇重大
          borrower; or a breach of contract, such as a default or                財 政 困 難； 或 違 約， 例 如
          past due event; or                                                     拖欠或逾期付款事件；或


   •      the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or                     •   借貸人的放貸人就經濟或
          contractual reasons relating to the borrower’s                         與借貸人財務困難有關的
          financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a                 合約理由給予借貸人在一
          concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise                   般情況下放貸人不予考慮
          consider; or                                                           之優惠條件；或


   •      it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter               •   借貸人可能破產或進行其
          bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation.                          他財務重組。


   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                        預期信貸虧損的計量及確認


   The measurement of expected credit losses is a function of                預期信貸虧損的計量的依據為違約
   the probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude        概率、違約損失率（即違約時的損
   of the loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.           失程度）及違約風險的函數。評估
   The assessment of the probability of default and loss given               違約概率及違約損失率乃依據過往
   default is based on historical data adjusted by forward-looking           數據，並按前瞻性資料調整。而金
   information. As for the exposure at default, for financial assets,        融資產的違約風險暴露則由資產於
   this is represented by the assets’ gross carrying amount at the           報告日期的賬面總值代表。
   reporting date.

             Measurement and recognition of ECL (Continued)                                       （續）
                                                                                       預期信貸虧損的計量及確認


             For financial assets, the expected credit loss is estimated as the        就金融資產而言，預期信貸虧損根
             difference between all contractual cash flows that are due to the         據合約應付本集團的所有合約現金
             Group in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows              流量與本集團預期將收取的所有現
             that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the original             金流量之間的差額估計，並按原來
             effective interest rate.                                                  的實際利率貼現。


             If the Group has measured the loss allowance for a financial              倘本集團於上個報告期間以等於可
             instrument at an amount equal to lifetime ECL in the previous             使用年期預期信貸虧損的金額計量
             reporting period, but determines at the current reporting date            一項金融工具的虧損撥備，但於本
             that the conditions for lifetime ECL are no longer met, the Group         報告日期釐定該可使用年期預期信
             measures the loss allowance at an amount equal to 12-month                貸虧損的條件不再符合，則本集團
             ECL at the current reporting date, except for assets for which            於本報告日期按相等於12個月預期
             simplified approach was used.                                             信貸虧損的金額計量虧損撥備（以
                                                                                       簡化方法計量的資產除外）。


             The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit                 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認本集團
             or loss for all financial instruments with a corresponding                於損益確認所有金融工具的減值收
             adjustment to their carrying amount.                                      入或虧損，對其賬面值作出相應調
                                                                                       整。


             Derecognition of financial assets                                         中止確認金融資產


             The Group derecognises a financial asset only when the                    本集團僅於從資產收取現金流的合
             contractual rights to the cash flows from the asset expire, or            約權利屆滿時，或向另一方轉讓金
             when it transfers the financial asset and substantially all the           融資產及該資產所有權之絕大部分
             risks and rewards of ownership of the asset to another party.             風險及回報時終止確認金融資產。
             If the Group neither transfers nor retains substantially all the          倘本集團並無轉移亦無保留擁有權
             risks and rewards of ownership and continues to control the               的絕大部分風險及回報並繼續控制
             transferred asset, the Group recognises its retained interest             已轉讓資產，本集團繼續按持續
             in the asset and an associated liability for amounts it may               參與的程度將資產確認入賬並確認
             have to pay. If the Group retains substantially all the risks and         相關負債。倘本集團保留已轉讓金
             rewards of ownership of a transferred financial asset, the Group          融資產擁有權的絕大部分風險及回
             continues to recognise the financial asset and also recognises            報，本集團繼續確認金融資產，並
             a collateralised borrowing for the proceeds received.                     就已收取的所得款項確認一項已抵
                                                                                       押借貸。

   Financial liabilities and equity instruments                                金融負債及權益工具
   Classification as debt or equity                                            分類為債務或權益

   Debt and equity instruments issued by the Group are classified              本集團發行之債務及權益工具乃根
   as either financial liabilities or as equity in accordance with the         據合同安排之內容以及金融負債及
   substance of the contractual arrangements and the definitions               權益工具之定義分類為金融負債或
   of a financial liability and an equity instrument.                          權益。

   Financial liabilities                                                       金融負債

   All financial liabilities are subsequently measured at amortised            所有金融負債其後採用實際利率法
   cost using the effective interest method.                                   按攤銷成本計量。

   Financial liabilities subsequently measured at amortised cost               其後按攤銷成本計量的金融負債

   Financial liabilities that are not 1) contingent consideration of           並非(1)業務合併中收購方的或然代
   an acquirer in a business combination, 2) held-for-trading, or 3)           價；(2)持作買賣；或(3)指定按公平
   designated as at fair value through profit or loss (“FVTPL”), are           值計入損益的金融負債，其後採用
   subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective                 實際利率法按攤銷成本計量。
   interest method.

   The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                實際利率法乃計算金融負債攤銷成
   amortised cost of a financial liability and of allocating interest          本及按相關期間攤分利息開支之方
   expense over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is            法。實際利率為在金融負債之預計
   the rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash payments              年期或（視適用情況而定）較短期
   (including all fees and points paid or received that form an                間，使估計未來現金支出（包括所
   integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and         有構成實際利率組成部分之已付或
   other premiums or discounts) through the expected life of the               已收費用及利率點、交易成本及其
   financial liability, or (where appropriate) a shorter period, to the        他溢價或折讓）準確貼現至一項金
   amortised cost of a financial liability.                                    融負債的攤銷成本之該利率。

             Impairment loss of financial assets                                       金融資產減值虧損


             Financial assets are assessed for indicators of impairment                金融資產於各報告期間結束時評估
             at the end of each reporting period. Financial assets are                 減值跡象。倘有客觀證據證明金融
             considered to be impaired where there is objective evidence               資產於初步確認後出現一項或多項
             that, as a result of one or more events that occurred after the           影響其估計未來現金流量之事宜，
             initial recognition of the financial asset, the estimated future          則金融資產被認為已減值。
             cash flows of the financial assets have been affected.


             Objective evidence of impairment could include:                           客觀減值證據可包括：


             •       significant financial difficulty of the issuer or                 •   發行人或交易方面對重大
                     counterparty; or                                                      財政困難；或


             •       breach of contract, such as default or delinquency in             •   違反合同，如利息及本金付
                     interest or principal payments; or                                    款出現逾期或拖欠情況；或


             •       it becoming probable that the borrower will enter                 •   借款人可能面臨破產或進
                     bankruptcy or financial re-organisation; or                           行財務重組；或


             •       disappearance of an active market for that financial              •   因財政困難導致該金融資
                     asset because of financial difficulties.                              產失去活躍市場。


             For certain categories of financial assets, such as trade and             就應收賬款及其他應收款項等若干
             other receivables, assets that are assessed not to be impaired            類別金融資產而言，個別評估時獲
             individually are, in addition, assessed for impairment on a               評為未有減值之資產將另行共同進
             collective basis. Objective evidence of impairment for a portfolio        行減值評估。有關應收款項組合減
             of receivables could include the Group’s past experience of               值之客觀證據可包括本集團過往收
             collecting payments, an increase in the number of delayed                 取款項之經驗、組合中已超過25日
             payments in the portfolio past the credit period ranging from 25          至 30 日信貸期之延誤付款數目增
             to 30 days, observable changes in national or local economic              加，以及與拖欠應收款項相關之國
             conditions that correlate with default on receivables.                    家或本地經濟狀況顯著變動。

   For financial assets carried at amortised cost, the amount of             就按攤銷成本入賬之金融資產而
   the impairment loss recognised is the difference between the              言，減值虧損金額指資產賬面值與
   asset’s carrying amount and the present value of the estimated            估計未來現金流量按金融資產之原
   future cash flows discounted at the financial asset’s original            有實際利率貼現之現值間之差額。
   effective interest rate.


   The carrying amount of the financial assets is reduced by                 所有金融資產之賬面值均直接減去
   the impairment loss directly for all financial assets with the            減值虧損，惟應收賬款及其他應收
   exception of trade and other receivables, where the carrying              款項除外，其賬面金額乃透過撥備
   amount is reduced through the use of an allowance account.                賬扣減。撥備賬之賬面值變動於損
   Changes in the carrying amount of the allowance amount are                益確認。倘應收賬款或其他應收款
   recognised in profit or loss. When trade and other receivables            項被評為無法收回，則有關款項與
   are considered uncollectible, they are written off against                撥備賬撇銷。其後收回過往撇銷之
   the allowance account. Subsequent recoveries of amounts                   款項將計入損益。
   previously written off are credited to profit or loss.


   For financial assets measured at amortised cost, if, in a                 就按攤銷成本計量之金融資產而
   subsequent period, the amount of impairment loss decreases                言，倘往後期間減值虧損金額減
   and the decrease can be related objectively to an event                   少，而有關減少客觀上與確認減值
   occurring after the impairment losses was recognised, the                 虧損後發生之事件有關，則先前確
   previously recognised impairment loss is reversed through                 認之減值虧損於損益撥回，惟該資
   profit or loss to the extent that the carrying amount of the asset        產於減值撥回當日之賬面值不得超
   at the date the impairment is reversed does not exceed what               過倘並無確認減值而原應存在之攤
   the amortised cost would have been had the impairment not                 銷成本。
   been recognised.

   Derecognition                                                            終止確認

   A financial asset is derecognised only when the contractual              僅當從資產收取現金流量之權利屆
   rights to the cash flows from the asset expire, or when it               滿時，或轉讓其金融資產及於金
   transfers the financial asset and substantially all the risks            融資產擁有權之絕大部分風險及回
   and rewards of ownership of the asset to another entity. If              報轉移予另一實體時，方終止確認
   the Group neither transfers nor retains substantially all the            該金融資產。倘本集團轉移或保留
   risks and rewards of ownership and continues to control the              擁有權絕大部分風險及回報並繼續
   transferred asset, the Group recognise its retained interest             控制已轉移資產，則本集團確認其
   in the asset and an associated liability for amounts it may              於資產之保留權益及可能需要支付
   have to pay. If the Group retains substantially all the risks and        之相關負債款項。倘本集團保留已
   rewards of ownership of a transferred financial asset, the Group         轉讓金融資產擁有權絕大部分風險
   continues to recognise the financial asset and also recognises           及回報，本集團可繼續確認金融資
   a collateralised borrowing for the proceeds received.                    產，亦可就已收取的所得款項確認
                                                                            已抵押借貸。


   On derecognition of a financial asset in its entirety, the               於終止確認其全部金融資產時，資
   difference between the asset’s carrying amount and the sum of            產賬面值與已收及應收代價及已於
   the consideration received and receivable and the cumulative             其他全面收入內確認及於權益累積
   gain or loss that had been recognised in other comprehensive             之累計損益之總和之差額將確認為
   income and accumulated in equity is recognised in profit or              損益。
   loss.


   A financial liability is derecognised when, and only when, the           本集團僅當本公司之責任獲解除
   Group’s obligations are discharged, cancelled or expired.                後、取消或到期時方終止確認金融
   The difference between the carrying amount of the financial              負債。終止確認之金融負債賬面值
   liability derecognised and the consideration paid and payable is         與已付或應付代價之差額確認為損
   recognised in profit or loss.                                            益。

             investment property portfolios and concluded that the Group’s              總結為本集團於中國的投資物業

             the economic benefits embodied in the investment property                  部分經濟利益的商業模式持有。因
             over time, rather than through sale. Therefore, in measuring               此，於計量本集團於該等投資物業
             the Group’s deferred taxation on investment properties, the                中的遞延稅項時，本公司董事釐定
             directors of the Company determined that the presumption                   以公允值模式計量的該等投資物業
             that the carrying amounts of investment properties measured                賬面值乃全部透過出售收回的假設
             using the fair value model recovered entirely through sale                 不成立。因此，本集團繼續按物業
             was rebutted. As a result, the Group continues to recognise                之全部賬面值將透過使用收回的假
             deferred tax liabilities on change in fair value of investment             設確認投資物業公允值變動之遞延
             properties on the basis that the entire carrying amounts of the            稅項負債。
             properties will be recovered through use.

             regime, the first HK$2 million of profits of qualifying corporation     二級利得稅稅率制度，合資格企業

             the management for the two-tiered profits tax rates regime              12 月 31 日止年度，本集團在香港
             and continue to be taxed at the flat rate of 16.5%. For the             的所有實體的溢利並無獲管理層選

   The subsidiaries in Hong Kong operate defined contribution           香港附屬公司為合資格僱員按香港
   schemes (the “MPF Scheme”) under the Hong Kong Mandatory             強制性公積金計劃條例推行定額供
   Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance which are available to              款計劃（「強積金計劃」）。該等計劃
   qualified employees. The assets of the schemes are held              之資產與附屬公司之資產於獨立管
   separately from those of the subsidiaries in independently           理基金內分開持有。附屬公司之每
   administered funds. Monthly contributions made by the                月供款乃按照有關規定所訂明以適
   subsidiaries are calculated based on certain percentages of          用薪酬成本之若干百分比或固定款
   the applicable payroll costs or fixed sums as stipulated under       額（視適用情況而定）計算。僱員須
   the relevant requirements, as appropriate. The employees             按其月薪之5%或最高1,500港元作
   are required to contribute 5% of their monthly salaries or up        出供款，而彼等可選擇作出額外供
   to a maximum of HK$1,500 and they can choose to make                 款。僱員於65歲退休、身故或完全
   additional contributions. The employees are entitled to 100% of      喪失工作能力時享有 100% 之僱主
   the employer’s Mandatory Contributions upon their retirement         強制性供款。
   at the age of 65, death or total incapacity.


   Pursuant to the regulations of the relevant authorities in the       根據中國有關當局之規例，本公司
   PRC, the subsidiaries of the Company in this country participate     之中國附屬公司參與各項政府退休
   in respective government retirement benefit schemes (the             福 利 計 劃（「 該 等 計 劃 」）， 據 此，
   “Schemes”) whereby the subsidiaries are required to contribute       該等附屬公司須向該等計劃作出供
   to the Schemes to fund the retirement benefits of the eligible       款，以撥付合資格僱員之退休福
   employees. Contributions made to the Schemes are calculated          利。向該等計劃作出之供款乃按照
   based on certain percentages of the applicable payroll costs         中國規例所訂明適用薪酬成本之特
   as stipulated under the requirements in the PRC. The relevant        定百分比計算。中國有關當局負責
   authorities of the PRC are responsible for the entire pension        向退休僱員支付應付之全數退休福
   obligations payable to the retired employees. The only               利。本集團就該等計劃之責任僅為
   obligation of the Group with respect to the Schemes is to pay        根據該等計劃持續作出所需供款。
   the ongoing required contributions under the Schemes.


   The retirement benefit schemes contribution represent gross          退休福利計劃供款即本集團向由中
   contributions by the Group to the Schemes operated by the            國有關當局運作之該等計劃以及於
   relevant authorities of the PRC and the defined contribution         香港運作之定額供款計劃作出之供
   schemes operated in Hong Kong.                                       款總額。
